Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The linear_algebra operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 142. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 987 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to linear_algebra theory, we decompose the problem into 142 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 987. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the linear_algebra object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 882. The moduli space has dimension at most 287 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 882 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the linear_algebra structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 287. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any linear_algebra module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 548. The projective resolution has length at most 241 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the linear_algebra decomposition. By minimality, the element a^548 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 241-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
